# D172 — BLAKE3 two-round quarter-step knowledge grid

Generated from `research/tools/traillab/ATLAS_BLAKE3.md` and validated `research/dig/CELLS.jsonl`; no BLAKE3 search or package build is performed by this generator.

## Status discipline

`I` is an exactly inverted/solved quarter-step, `P` a pinned/advice value, `0` a proved zero difference, `F` an exact XOR consequence, `$` a paid filter/cube/solver condition, `L` a listed subset of an exact outcome partition, and `Z` a killed door. Evidence status is separate: BEDROCK/COMPUTED/SAMPLED/LOCAL_ONLY/CLAIMED-UNTESTED/KILLED/OPEN.

The record has 16 G calls (8 per round), four quarter-steps each: q1=(a1,d1), q2=(c1,b1), q3=(a2,d2), q4=(c2,b2). The table below explicitly expands to all 64 quarter-steps, followed by the output fold.

## Closed/open boundaries

- No W22-style fake freedom appears: Lemma T2 is a bijection onto admissible blocks.
- Adding unlisted beta outcomes loses at the current ledger because 83% of cost is typed omega-list words; this is the reverse of the SHA-256 printed-cell gap.
- The third-beta row adds zero typed words and has a measured rate gain ceiling around 0.010 bit; it is not a free rate claim.
- The coupled omega list is the main unclosed lossless candidate (sample c=0.866); per-phase exact counting and list-build cost are still required.
- Factor 0.974, lambda, stream/replica memory rules, and G5 alternatives retain their recorded premises/statuses; no outside claim is promoted.

'Necessary' = equal b/d outputs are not a property of random constants (random replacement 0/2000) and the pin is exactly the cube above; a single-coordinate scan is a local test around the record, not the global solution count (global: 64 pins per realizable cell, 8 cells, record profile; see the Q4 rows). In-call mass 2^64 per base vs 1 for zero difference (2^64-2^32 with a1'!=a1, 2^32-1 with a1'=a1 where y'=y and dA=-dx, so Y0 must differ); message-only (A,D equal) nontrivial members: 0 (0/60000 random, never 2 outputs) | research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work/b3e0.py; research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work/run_exps.py; research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work/results.json; research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work/run_exps.out | Universe cell blake3-r2/E0.q1/step/Q2. Relaxing q1 is the only quarter-level freedom the event gives E0. Own tracer b3e0.py validated first: BLAKE3('') 7 rounds = af1349b9..., package Fact P C3 values, Lemma L (2000), Ginv round trip. Clean reachable bases = pinned X3,X7,X11,X15, W4/W4', W13, w14=w15=0: 20000/20000 have Y differing only at words 3,11, identical E0 inputs, fold words 0,2,5,7 equal (Lemma H reproduced). Exact in-call fact (bijection of G, Fact 1 backward): with the four E0 outputs held equal, B'=B and C'=C are forced and the whole absorbing family is 2^64 per base: (x',y') free, then a1'=a2-b1-y', D'=ROL(d1,16)^a1', A'=a1'-B-x', i.e. dD=a1^a1' and y'=y+a1-a1'; checked forward on 100000 preimage trials and 20000 constructive trials (all four outputs equal every time). Every non-trivial member has (dw1,dw11)!=(0,0). Activation (generic words, 2-round pair, unpinned so E0/E2 show active in the baseline): baseline has 1 of the 11 calls K0..K3,D0..D3,C0..C2 active (K2); adding dw1 activates 9 of them (all 16 X words differ); dw11 alone 5 (D1,K2,C0,C1,C2; 4 X words); the E0-absorbing pair (dw1=+d,dw11=-d) 9; best case found, K0 absorbed by a counter-low difference (dw0=-d, dt=a1^a1', K0 outputs equal 200/200) leaves 6 active (K0,K2,D1,C0,C1,C2) because D1 cannot absorb dw11 on an identical state. Digest-fold words 0,2,5,7 equal in 0 of every 200-400 relaxed pairs; residual-0 solution: none found, existence NOT decided (no joint solve of the upstream feeders was run). Cross cancellation E0 vs E2 (event-level alternative, z0~z8, z5~z13, z10~z2, z15~z7): E0 varies (w1,w11) 2^22 draws, E2 varies w9 only (w14=0), 3 bases: single-word matches 4021..4204 vs 4096 expected, two-word matches 0 vs 1e-6 expected; 128 bits needed vs 2^96 freedom = 2^-32 per base heuristic.  LOCAL_ONLY: window = E0 call on clean round-1 states plus 2-round activation sampling; no full-path joint solve. No gain found. |

Validated first: own compress == organizer verifier/blake3.py on 1000 single-block messages at 2 and 7 rounds, empty-message 7-round vector af1349b9... reproduced, and the h=IV builder reproduces pins X3/X7/X11/X15, Y4=y, X equal and digest words 0,2,5,7 equal on 3000/3000 (also through verifier.blake3 on the 55/63-byte strings). Why: h[0] enters round 0 only as the a-input of K0 (v0), whose call is solved backwards, so it only shifts the derived message word (w0 = K0a1 - h0 - h4); the round-0 target state X, the pins and C3 (Fact P) do not see it, and the digest never XORs h (256-bit output is Z[i]^Z[i+8]). Free-parameter count unchanged (same 8 parameters, same bijection). The residual R=0 mass itself (2^-128 scale) cannot be sampled; the measured proxy matches the h=IV control within sampling noise, so the exact outcome mass is LOCAL_ONLY. Round 1 reads that message word again, so the block moves but the pair structure is unchanged. Own code only, pkgB3U root construction at 2 rounds. |

With bit 3 = 1 the length-cancelling partner is W4-8 instead of W4+8, the C3 pin (Fact P) no longer holds and digest words 0,2,5,7 differ. Outcome mass: half of the h[2] values keep the pinned pair and the per-trial mass of h=IV (low-6-bit agreement of digest-word XOR difference for words 1,3,4,6 = 269/443/287/232 of 20000 (control h=IV: 276/495/334/231)); the other half lose the whole event unless W4 and the pins are re-solved | research/dig/incoming2/blake3-r2-G05-U01/work/initlib.py; research/dig/incoming2/blake3-r2-G05-U01/work/val.py; research/dig/incoming2/blake3-r2-G05-U01/work/scan_init.py; research/dig/incoming2/blake3-r2-G05-U01/work/scan_init.out.json; research/dig/incoming2/blake3-r2-G05-U01/work/w4scan.c; research/dig/incoming2/blake3-r2-G05-U01/work/w4scan.out.txt | Scan of universe cell blake3-r2/INIT.v2/INIT/Q2 . Mechanism: K = h2+h6 (5bf2cd1d at h=IV) gives w4' = ((K+w4) xor 8) - K, which is w4+8 iff bit 3 of K+w4 is 0 (K+W4 = f33a2355, bit 3 = 0). Pins were solved for the pair (W4, W4+8), so they stay valid exactly when that bit is 0. This is a 1-bit necessity on the value of h[2]+h[6], not a cost: any h[2] with that bit 0 is free. Residual R=0 mass at 2^-128 not sampled, so LOCAL_ONLY. |

Z2<-Y7 and Z10<-Y15 by birthday sampling of 2^23 inputs: 16458 and 16243 output collisions vs 8192 for an injective map and about 16384 for a random function (sampled). Reading: the pin is necessary for the 4-word bundle (0,2,5,7 share Y7,Y15), not for word 0 alone. Z0<-Y15 (E0 a-output, d input) is an exact bijection, so Y15 alone cannot cancel. Why: v14 is read only by K2 and v15 only by K3; K3 cancellation puts the difference on w6 (C0 y-slot) and w7 (C2 x-slot). A y-only difference in a G call changes all four outputs (proved: a2 differs so d2, c2, b2 differ; 0 equal outputs in 1e6 random calls), so C0 cannot be pinned, Y0,Y4,Y8,Y12 differ and every diagonal E0..E3 reads one of them. So the clean pair E0/E2 that the length line buys is lost as soon as a flags (or any other K3) difference is added or substituted. Model is word-level with the rule: no input difference = clean, x-only difference = outputs b,d pinnable, anything else = all outputs differ; it is optimistic for the attacker (assumes every pinnable call is pinned). LOCAL_ONLY: roots with h=IV and free message words only; chunk-block roots with a CV and parent roots with child-CV words are not covered. Matches the desk claim in CLOSED_DOORS 21/22 (8 conditions) and tighten/b3_parent_node_scout section 3.1 case B; now measured on our code. |
| `C0287` | COMPUTED | yes | Cancelling an XOR difference delta on a call input d (v14 length, v15 flags) needs w' = ((K+w) xor delta) - K with K = h_a + h_b; the resulting additive step Delta = (u xor delta) - u, u = K + w, equals the sum over bits i of delta of (1 - 2 u_i) 2^i exactly (checked on 100,000 random (u,delta)). Number of carry classes = 2^popcount(delta), each class 2^(32 - popcount) values of u: length 55^63 = 8: 2 classes {+8,-8}, 2^31 each; flags 11^12 = 7: 8 classes {+-1+-2+-4}, 2^29 each; flags 11^10 = 1: 2 classes, 2^31 each; length L^64 for L=40: delta 104, 8 classes, 2^29 each; L=55: delta 119, 64 classes, 2^26 each. A/C needs K2 and K3 cancelled together: classes multiply (55 vs parent: 64 x 8 = 512 class pairs). Free message words: A/A 14 of 16 (w14=w15=0 for honest bytes), A/C 12 of 16 on the free side; the parent side has 16 child-CV words, not free. | research/dig/incoming2/blake3-r2-G05-U10/work/carries.py; research/dig/incoming2/blake3-r2-G05-U10/work/carries.out.json | universe cell blake3-r2/MODE.tree/MODE/Q3. Counts are exact (enumerated over the delta bit patterns; bits of u outside delta do not matter). They count how many different additive steps w4'-w4 (or w6'-w6) the cancellation can produce, which decides whether a pinned downstream call (here C3 pinned for Delta = +8 only) can be reused: only the 55^63 class +8 reaches the existing pin set. The free-word count for A/C covers only the side where the message is a raw 64-byte block. |

## D172 conclusion

The BLAKE3 two-round record has 64 construction/output quarter-steps represented above. Its only deliberate stricter-than-event choice is the listed outcome set; the exact residual partition itself has no SHA-style hidden multiplicity. The principal remaining mathematical lever is a lossless coupled omega-list projection, presently sampled but not exact or priced.

Sources: `research/tools/traillab/ATLAS_BLAKE3.md`, `research/tools/traillab/b3atlas/`, `research/dig/CELLS.jsonl`, and the pkgB3U/B3M proof and ledger records named there.
